# RH CARD — Card Illustration Prompts (fantasy/heroic direction, v3)

One image-generation prompt per card (205 total, #1 PeopleSpheres +
#2–205 across the 12 domains), for an external image model (Midjourney,
DALL-E, Gemini/"nano banana", Stable Diffusion, etc.), used one at a time
(paste a single card's prompt, generate, repeat). Generate each image, then
drop the result at `art/<card-id>.jpg` (preferred — much lighter than PNG
for this kind of painterly art; `.png`/`.webp` also work) and add the id to
`REAL_ART_IDS` in `js/data/cardart.js` — the game automatically prefers the
real image over the generative placeholder for that card, and falls back to
the placeholder automatically if the file is ever missing or fails to load.

For the 12 hero-portrait images (one per domain, used for the player/AI
avatar badge rather than a specific card), see `hero-portrait-prompts.md`.

**Changelog v3** (full redo, every card): each domain now has exactly 17
cards, each of which draws from three axes sized 1:1 to the domain's card
count — **17 unique signature body-details, 17 unique backdrops, 17 unique
nicknames** — assigned so no two cards in the same domain ever repeat the
same physical detail, setting, or title (pose/prop still cycle through a
smaller varied pool, since the detail+backdrop axes alone already guarantee
no two cards look alike). Every card also gets its own **unique secondary
accent color**, computed as a deterministic hue/lightness shift off the
domain's base color — so the domain's hue still anchors it visually to its
6-domain or 12-domain family, but no two cards in the whole 205-card set
share the exact same two-color palette. The gameplay-derived visual
flourish (v2's main improvement) is kept as-is.

## Art direction (shared style guide — keep every card consistent)

- **Concept**: each card is reimagined as a **heroic fantasy character** —
  a champion, a construct/golem, or a monster/beast — not a literal software
  product. The real SIRH editor name only survives as a **fantasy nickname**
  ("PayFit, the Swift Paymaster") and a one-line in-universe legend; the
  *visual* is 100% fantasy trading-card art (think Magic: The Gathering /
  Hearthstone splash art), never a screenshot, logo, or corporate scene.
- **No literal logos, wordmarks, or real-world branding** anywhere in the
  image, and **no readable text**.
- **Three archetypes**: 🦸 Hero (humanoid champion), 🗿 Golem/Construct
  (magical machine or animated statue), 🐉 Monster/Beast (creature).
- **Domain identity carries through materials, not logos** (coins/ledgers
  for Paie/GA, shields/gears for GTA, bows/compasses for Recrutement,
  vines/growth for Formation, lightning/energy for Talent-Performance,
  charts/data-nodes for Pilotage/BI).
- **Palette**: the domain's hex color drives armor trim / glow / hide
  markings, kept consistent across the set.
- **Composition**: dynamic pose, dramatic rim lighting, painterly digital
  illustration (not photorealistic, not 3D render, not flat vector).
- **Detail scales with rarity**: cleaner for 1★ commons, increasingly epic
  for 3★ and the legendary card.
- **Mood**: heroic, awe-inspiring, a little larger-than-life.

## Format — read this before generating (avoids layout bugs in-game)

The same image gets cropped by CSS `object-fit: cover` into **two very
different shapes** depending on where a card is shown, so a bad format
choice will cut off heads/subjects in one of them:

- A **wide banner strip** at the top of the card-tile / hand-card / zoom
  views — roughly **2:1 to 2.2:1 landscape**.
- A **near-square full-bleed background** behind the mini/tiny card view
  (with a dark gradient scrim over the bottom for text) — roughly **5:6
  portrait** (close to square).

**Generate every image as a 1:1 square, at least 1024×1024px**, with the
character centered, fully visible, and reasonably close-in (not tiny in
the middle of a huge empty scene) so the vertical-center band still reads
well once cropped into the landscape banner. A square crops cleanly to
both shapes without losing the subject.

⚠️ **Do not describe this as a "safe zone," a percentage, or any kind of
grid/box** — several test generations rendered that instruction as a
literal visible rectangle/frame overlaid on the artwork. Describe the
framing purely in plain artistic terms (as done below), and explicitly
tell the model **not to render any frame, border, grid, crop-marks,
watermark, or UI overlay** — the output must be pure illustration, nothing
else. This constraint is repeated inside every individual prompt below so
each one is paste-ready on its own.

Each entry gives: real name + fantasy nickname (context only), archetype,
domain + hex color, rarity, one-line legend, ability context, and the
ready-to-paste prompt.

## Légendaire

### 1. PeopleSpheres — *PeopleSpheres, l'Unificateur des Douze Domaines*  `peoplespheres`
- 👑 Déité unificatrice · Domaine : Transversal (#1c1c28) · Rareté : ∞
- Légende : PeopleSpheres ne choisit pas de camp : elle unit tous les domaines — et toutes les créatures qui les incarnent — sous une seule bannière.
- Contexte gameplay (inspiration uniquement) : Effet d'Entrée : Synchronisation Universelle : pour chaque domaine différent en jeu, +1/+1/+1, piochez 1 carte et soignez 1 PV. Compte comme membre des 12 domaines pour les synergies.
- **Prompt** :

> Premium fantasy trading-card splash art, painterly digital illustration. A radiant, transcendent hero-deity figure standing at the convergence of twelve glowing pathways, one per allied race it unifies: an elf's golden coin-light, a troll's steel-shield gleam, a ninja-scout's arrow-trail, a wizard's blooming vine-light, a dragon's crackling lightning, an owl-sage's streaming data-light, a stone golem's teal warning-glow, a djinn's violet contract-smoke, a goblin's amber coin-shimmer, a ghost archivist's pale sage-green wisp, a fairy's magenta travel-spark, and a digital spider's cool blue circuitry-glow — their form and armor woven from all twelve motifs merging into one being, faint silhouettes of each race's leader visible within the folds of their radiant cloak. Dominant color accent: deep near-black indigo (#1c1c28) with luminous glints from all twelve domain colors. legendary-tier grandeur: the most epic, elaborate, and luminous composition in the set, dramatic god-rays, sweeping scale. Dramatic god-rays, sweeping heroic pose, no readable text, no logos, no real-world branding, no photorealism, no 3D render — a unifying "platform deity" that towers above every other card in the set. Format: square 1:1 image, at least 1024x1024px. The character stands centered and fully visible, filling a good portion of the frame, with open background/atmosphere around them (not tiny in a vast empty scene). Absolutely no visible frame, border, rectangle, grid, crop-marks, watermark, signature, or any UI-like overlay anywhere in the image — pure illustration only, nothing else drawn on top of it.
